# IX. Report by the Board of Directors

### 4 Profit distribution proposal for year 2016

Audited and confirmed by Qinghai Dazheng CPA Co., Ltd., net profit realized by the Company for year 2016 is 709,289,654.20 Yuan. According to relevant laws, rules and regulations and the Articles of Association, the after-tax profit for year 2016 is distributed as follows:

(1) 10% of the audited net profit for year 2016 is withdrawn as statutory surplus public reserve, and 70,928,965.42 Yuan is withdrawn;

(2) According to relevant rules and regulations by the Ministry of Finance, based on balance of risk assets at the end of the year 2016, general risk reserve 200,000,000 Yuan;

(3) With 1,861,391,038 shares as the base, 0.15 Yuan is distributed for each share, and cash 279,208,655.70 Yuan is distributed;

(4) Remaining profit retention will be distributed in the following years.

### 5 Work plan of the Board of Directors for Year 2017

In the year 2017, under several pressures, combining the state's reform of developing supply-side reform and active financial policy and stable currency policy, the Company compensates its disadvantages in reform and innovation, places risk prevention and control at a more crucial position, based on controlling downward business performance in year 2016, and continues to realize the a relatively stable development objective.

##### Main business objectives for year 2017:

1. Net profit will be 750 million Yuan;

2. Non-performing loan rate below 2%;

3. Net assets yield is more than 12%;

4. All supervision indexes are conforming.

##### Work report by the Board of Directors:

##### (1) Fully implement the development strategy under new situations

To take Qinghai Province as the first inclusive financial model area in China as opportunity, implement the philosophy of “four transformations” by provincial Party Committee at the Bank of Qinghai, and fully implement development strategy under new situations. Firstly, based on developing the inclusive finance, ecofriendly finance and mobile finance, adhere to the principle of unswerving real economy, the targeted poverty relieving, determine the system, assignment and proposals, combine its own development into the inclusive finance model area, and provide financial service for the supply-side structure reform; secondly, based on the lean management, centering the advantageous industry direct investment, small and pretty-enterprise loan, agricultural load, consumption loan and poverty relieving loan, further perfect the management system, optimize organization structure and business structure, actively explore the new development room from the supply-side structure reform, actively interface new requirements from economic structure adjustment, and lead the business to the origin of bank business.

##### (2) Perfect practical corporate governance structure

Taken deepened reform and development requirements of state-owned enterprises into account, actively explore new requirements of governance structure construction of state-owned enterprises in the new period, reinforce communication and coordination mechanism with the Party Committee of the Bank of Qinghai, take the party building into the corporate governance structure, further clarify duty boundaries of the Party Committee, the Board

# IX. Report by the Board of Directors

of Directors, the Board of Supervisors and the business tier, fully yield the advantages of mixing ownership equity structure, and establish the modern state-owned enterprise juristic person governance structure with Party Committee as the political core, the Board of Directors with power of decision-making, the Board of Supervisors with function of supervision and the management with their respective duties.

##### (3) Reinforce scientific decision-making of the Board of Directors

In the year 2017, the Board of Director researches and knows about overall development situations of the Board of Directors, fully plays professionalism and decision-making performance of all directors, and advances coordinated and balance development of management, quality, benefits and scale of the Bank of Qinghai. Firstly, reinforce capital management, on the basis of increasing capital and expanding shares for the Bank of Qinghai consented by the provincial government, carefully research and draft multi-channel capital supplementation plan, ensure powerful support for business development by capital, ensure that capital increasing and the risk asset increasing are matched, guide the operation and manager tier to implement lean management under capital restriction, change the conventional development mode of high capital consumption and extensive operation; secondly, fully support business and management by the business and manager tier, supervise work of the business and management tier in terms of business innovation and development, risk prevention and control achievements and management performance improvement, and ensure that the annual business plan is completed successfully; thirdly, actively play the role of the final person responsible for risk prevention and control by the Board of Directors, supervise that the business and manager tier operates safely and there are no incidents throughout the year; fourthly, continue to reinforce reporting and communication with competent leaders and try to renewal of the 4th session of Board of Directors and Board of Supervisors; fifthly, actively analyze feasibility of equity investment of financial enterprises, and make progress of equity investment of financial enterprises.

##### (4) Fully reinforce risk management and internal control system

In the year 2017, continue to adhere to stay at the baseline, solve key issues and perfect systems, further specify responsibilities of risk prevention and control by the business management tier, place requirements on risk management in terms of system execution, supervision in place and proper handling, and ensure stable and healthy development of the bank. Firstly, reinforce overall risk management building, organize and supplement the existing internal control structures and systems, and enhance comprehensiveness and professionalism of risk management; secondly, determine to handle a batch of risk points, in particular to assets with significant single amount and high breaching ratio, draft practical handling proposals and ensure that the non-conforming assets are under control; thirdly, continue to increase accrual of assets depreciation reserve.

##### (5) Continuously deepen talent mechanism and system building

In the year 2017, on the basis of the “Thirteenth Five-year Plan” development program and talent development program by the Bank of Qinghai, continue to inherit the talent philosophy of “human-centered, fully yielding intelligence and capabilities”, fully utilize talent policy platform provided by the provincial Party Committee and the provincial government, reinforce state-owned enterprise HR management and improve talent team quality. Manage training and introduction of intermediate and high-end talents, continuously regulate and perfect staff team, build a leader and staff team with sufficient quantity, proper structure and high quality, try to be reformation base for local financial talent system and mechanism and key carrier for training and introduction of local financial talents, and provide powerful HR support and guarantee for core competitiveness and sustainable healthy development of the Bank of Qinghai.

#### 十、监事会报告

### 1 2016年监事会工作情况

(1) 持续强化风险管控工作。根据本公司风险现状，重点加强对信用风险、流动性风险、操作风险和声誉风险的关注和监督力度，进一步提高风险防控水平。同时，紧紧围绕2016年本公司中心工作，将风险管控作为生命线，立足全面风险管控、服务本公司快速稳健发展的宗旨，不断强化重点领域风险防控工作，持续关注政府平台、房地产、高载能企业贷款风险及风险抵补准备；加强案件防控和流动性风险管理工作的监督，密切关注负面舆情影响与化解工作，做到了风险管控与业务发展同安排、同部署，确保了本公司经营安全稳健。

(2) 认真开展履职评价工作。2016年，监事会充分发挥“观察员”和“监督员”作用，按照规定，开展了对本公司董事及高管人员的履职评价工作。全面考核董事诚实守信、勤勉尽职、合规履职方面的表现；考核高级管理人员在业务技能、处理复杂问题以及长期承诺和责任心方面的表现。重点监督董事在决策和风险管控方面的履职行为；监督高级管理人员在推进业务转型和精细化管理执行力方面的履职行为。完善对董事、高级管理人员履职情况的评价报告制度，多角度对董事和高级管理人员履职情况进行考察和监督。

(3) 扎实做好内部审计工作。2016年，监事会会同董事会指导内部审计部门在抓好业务薄弱环节开展常规检查的基础上，及时创新审计思路，积极拓展审计种类，进一步实现了审计工作覆盖到业务经营的各个领域，全辖首次开展了资管业务专项审计、任期经济责任审计、信息科技外包风险管理情况专项审计和信息科技服务合同履行情况专项审计。同时，委托审计部对全辖各分支行及机关部室工作情况开展了内控评价工作，以及全面开展银行业“两个加强、两个遏制”回头看专项检查。持续建立健全违规问责机制，加大问题整改落实力度，确保内部审计工作取得显著成效。全年对全辖各分支行及相关业务部门深入开展了29项常规审计、专项审计及特色审计。

(4) 全面加强财务管理工作。密切关注和监督影响财务收益的各项指标的执行情况，重点监督检查经营层对董事会核批的财务预算执行情况；监督检查各分支机构对总行下达财务收支计划执行情况及核拨费用的使用与管理情况；监督检查账务核算与年度利润的真实性；对重大项目支出实施专项监督检查，并提出改进提升财务管理的意见和建议。

(5) 持续推进自身建设。将监事会工作融入到日常经营管理工作中，充分利用和发挥内审部门、外部审计机构和监管部门的监督检查成果，推进持续跟踪整改落实。进一步完善工作评价报告制度，密切加强与董事会、经营管理层及监管部门的工作沟通与协调，全力支持和配合党委、董事会、经营层的工作，共同促进本公司持续稳健发展。2016年，本公司监事会按照《青海银行章程》规定，以现场和通讯方式共组织召开了4次监事会会议，审议了涉及年度报告、财务预决算方案、利润分配草案以及监事会相关工作报告等9项议案，听取重大报告8项。参会监事认真审议各项议案，从维护本公司利益和全体股东合法权益的角度出发，会前认真研阅会议议题，会中认真审议并充分发表意见，会后关注议案的实施落地情况，切实履行了监事职责。同时，监事会还通过出席股东大会、列席董事会和高级管理层会议，对重大决策及经营管理过程的合法合规性进行实时监督，对各类议题进行深入交流与讨论，提出建设性意见和建议，充分发挥了会议监督作用。

### 2 监事会就有关事项发表的独立意见

报告期内，根据本公司章程及中国银监会相关要求，本公司监事会通过与董事、高管人员访谈并列席董事会议、高管层经营会议及日常工作观察，按照有关股份制商业银行董事、高管人员履职尽职要求，对董事、高管人员2016年度履职情况作出如下评价意见：

(1) 监事会认为：2016年，董事会对宏观经济形势研判准确，对监管政策的解读到位，发展目标制定的合理、科学。结合实际，从转型发展、制度建设、风险控制、机构设置及高管配备等多方面依照股东大会授予的权限充分发挥作用。一是根据监管机构要求及本公司自身发展需要，不断完善公司治理结构，能够自觉接受监事会的监督。在准确研判内外形势，正确把握发展方向的同时，科学制定战略决策，破冰前行，筹划三年多的第四

#### 十、监事会报告

次增资工作获得省政府批准。全行上下改革开放意识不断增强，步伐明显加快，效果持续显现，在机构建设、社区银行、经营转型、同业合作、对外开放等领域不断迈出新步伐。二是全体董事能够严格按照法律法规、《青海银行章程》及有关规定的要求，本着对本公司及全体股东忠诚和勤勉尽责的态度，在密切关注本公司经营管理和重大战略发展项目实施情况，及时掌握风险状况的基础上，认真履行董事职责，积极维护本公司和全体股东的利益，关注存款人和中小股东的合法权益不受损害。及时参加董事会及其各专门委员会会议，客观地审议相关事项并做出科学的判断或合理的决策，为本公司的发展做出了积极的贡献。同时，全体董事能够严格树立良好的个人品行，充分做到依法行政、合规履职，不存在因个人原因影响董事会的正常决策或给本公司带来任何负面影响的行为。

(2) 监事会认为：2016年，本公司高级管理人员认真贯彻执行监管机构的监管要求，严格执行董事会、监事会决议，主动适应宏观经济增长乏力，利率市场化进程加快，以及去产能去库存降成本政策的影响，不断提升基层效能、推进集约化经营、补齐发展短板、深化改革创新，全年实现了资产安全风险防控底线不突破，重大案件不发生的目标，保持了各项监管指标全部达标。

### 3 2017年监事会工作重点

(1) 继续加强对风险管控工作的监督。要继续按照“比以往任何时候更加关注风险管控化解工作”的总体要求，树立底线思维，强化危机意识，密切关注并监督关乎本公司发展的风险控制状况，进一步建立健全全面风险管理体系，监督董事会和经营层在重大问题上风险防控的政策、程序和措施的落实执行情况。重点监督经营层在整个经营过程中风险防控措施的落实执行情况。突出审慎管理理念，进一步强化对案件防控、流动性风险和业务连续性工作的监督，促进全行安全稳健运营。加强对财务管理行为的监督，重点监督检查经营层对董事会核批的财务预算执行情况，各分支机构对总行下达财务收支计划执行情况及核拨费用的使用与管理情况及年度利润的真实性，加强对重大项目支出的监督检查。

(2) 继续加强对董事、高级管理人员履职情况的考核监督。进一步改进对董事、经营层高管人员履职情况的评价报告制度，多角度观察、监督、评价履职情况，提出监督评价意见和工作改进建议。加强与董事、高级管理人员的充分沟通，全力支持董事会和经营层开展工作，积极为全行工作建言献策，共同推动本公司经营管理水平再上新台阶。

(3) 继续加强对重大、重要政策实施落实情况的监督。对董事会制定的发展战略和风险控制策略的科学性、合理性和有效性进行评估，围绕新常态下经济社会发展的外部形势、金融领域改革的总体步调和本公司改革发展的内在条件，重点关注新一轮增资扩股方案的资本补充工作、消费金融公司挂牌运营等重大战略事项的推进情况。关注经营管理工作，推进业务转型和精细化管理，重点考察中小微金融业务、个人金融业务转型政策的落地实施情况、绩效管理政策的落实及其效应情况以及信息科技治理、安全运行、维护管理情况。

(4) 继续加强监事会监督效能。发挥求真务实精神，把监事会工作与日常经营管理工作紧密结合起来，以强化自我约束机制为切入口，以组织专项审计、检查、调查、跟踪检查等手段，不断改善工作方法，提高工作实效。

(5) 继续加强监事会自身建设。找准新常态下监事会工作的落脚点。根据国资委及监管部门对公司治理体系的新要求，进一步完善监事会组织架构，明确监事会职责权限，建立健全各项监督管理制度，对监事履职情况进行考核评价。注重与行党委、董事会和高级管理层的沟通协作，加强与总行各部门、各分支机构的联系，发挥好“兼听、谏言”作用。另外，加强与监管部门汇报沟通，争取监管部门的业务指导和工作支持，认真听取监管意见，引进先进理念，提高自身能力，进一步提高监事会的业务能力和专业水平。

## X. Report by the Board of Supervisors

### 1 Information of work by the Board of Supervisors in 2016

(1) Continue to reinforce risk management and control. According to current risk conditions of the Company, focus on concern and supervision of credit risks, liquidity risks, operation risks and reputation risks, and further improve risk control. Meanwhile, based on the Company's centering work in the year 2016, with risk management and control as the lifeline, keep a foothold of risk management and control, serve the tenet of the Company's quick and healthy development, continuously reinforce prevention and control in key fields, and continue to focus on loan risk and risk compensation preparations for government platform, real estate, high energy consumption enterprises; reinforce supervision over case prevention and control, and liquidity risk management, closely focus on negative public sentiment influences and dissolving, simultaneously arrange and deploy risk management control and business development, and ensure safe, stable and healthy development of the Company.

(2) Carefully carry out duty performance assessment. In the year 2016, the Board of Supervisors play the role of “observer” and “supervisor”, and assess performance of the Company’s directors and senior officers. Fully assess performance of directors in sincerity, due diligence and conforming duties; and assess performance of senior officers in business skills, tackling complicated issues, long-term commitment and responsibilities. Supervisor performance of directors in decision-making and risk management control; and supervise performance of senior officers in advancing business transformation and lean management. Perfect assessment report system by directors and senior managers, and assess and supervise performance of directors and senior managers in multiple views.

(3) Well manage internal audit. In the year 2016, on the basis of conducting regular check over weak procedures, the Board of Supervisors with the Board of Directors guide the internal audit department to innovate in audit, actively expand audit and extend audit to each and every field of business, and the audit covers special audit for assets management, economic duty audit during the tenure, special audit for information science& technology outsourcing management and performance of information science& technology service contract. The Audit Department is consigned to carry out internal control assessment on work of branches, sub-branches and administration, and the "two reinforcements, and two containments" review check of bank industry is carried out. Continue to establish and perfect the breaching accountability mechanism, increase the strength of correction of problems, and ensure that internal audit is effective. Perform 29 regular audits, special audits and featuring audits for branches, sub-branches and involving business departments throughout the year.

(4) Reinforce financial management. Closely focus on and supervise execution of indexes affecting financial proceeds, and supervise execution of financial budget by the operation tier verified and approved by the Board of Directors; supervise and check execution of financial revenues and expenditures and use and management of allocated funds; supervise and check authenticity of accounting and annual profits; perform special supervision and check over key projects, and propose opinions and suggestions on improving financial management.

(5) Continue to advance its own building. Combining work of the Board of Supervisors into daily operation and management, fully utilize and yield supervision check of internal audit department, external audit body and supervision authorities, and advance continued tracking, correction and implementation. Further perfect the work assessment and report system, closely reinforce communication and coordination with the Board of Directors, operation and management tier and supervision authorities, fully support and cooperate with work by the Party Committee, Board of Directors and operation tier and promote the Company's continuous, stable and healthy development. In the year 2016, according to Articles of Association of the Bank of Qinghai, the Board of Supervisors convoked 4 conferences of the Board of Supervisors in field and through communication, reviewed 9 proposals on annual report, financial budget and final settlement proposal, profit distribution proposal and the work report by the Board of Directors, and took 8 key

# X. Report by the Board of Supervisors

reports. The participating supervisors carefully reviewed the proposals, in view of safeguarding the Company's rights and interests and all shareholders' all legal rights and interests, researched and read conference proposals before the conference, reviewed and released opinions during the conference and performed duties of supervisors. Meanwhile, the Board of Supervisors supervised conformity and legality of key decisions, operation and management process through attending conferences of the Shareholders' Assembly, the Board of Supervisors and senior officer without voting, communicated and discusses various subjects, proposed constructive opinions and suggestions and fully played the role of conference supervision.

### 2 Independent opinions by the Board of Supervisors on relevant issues

During the reporting period, according to the Articles of Association and relevant requirements by China Banking Regulatory Commission, through interviewing directors and senior officers and attending conference of the Board of Directors and senior officers without voting and daily operation observation, based on due diligence requirements on directors and senior officers of joint stock commercial banks, the Board of Supervisors gives the following comments on performance of directors and senior officers in the year 2016:

(1) The Board of Supervisors comments as follows: in the year 2016, judgment over macroscopic economic situations by the Board of Directors was accurate, interpretation of the supervision policy was in place, and the development objectives were reasonable and scientific. Combined with reform and development, system building, risk control and senior officer, the authorities authorized by the Shareholders' Assembly worked well. Firstly, according to requirements by the supervision authorities and the Company's development needs, perfect the corporate governance structure and accept supervision of the Board of Supervisors. While researching and judging situations home and abroad and knowing about the right way, draft scientific decision-making scientifically, move forward as if walking on ice, and the 4th capital increasing which has been prepared for more than 3 years is approved by the provincial government. The awareness of reform and opening up throughout the bank has been intensified, the progress has been accelerated, effects are evidence, and new achievements have been made in body construction, community bank, business reform, peer cooperation, door-opening policy and other fields. Secondly, strictly in accordance with laws, the Articles of Association of the Bank of Qinghai and relevant rules and regulations, on the basis of being loyal to the Company and all company shareholders and the attitude of diligence, all the directors closely focus on implementation of the Company's business, management and key strategy development, timely know about risk control, carefully perform director duties, actively safeguard the Company's and all shareholders' rights and interests, and ensure that legal rights and interests of the depositors and medium and small shareholders will not be impaired. Timely participate in conferences of the Board of Directors and the special committees, objectively review relevant issues and make scientific judgment or reasonable decision-making, and actively contribute to the Company's development. Meanwhile, all directors have well individual characters, exercise work and duties according to laws, without decision-making by the Board of Directors affected due to personal reasons or actions without negative influences on the Company.

(2) The Board of Supervisors comment that: in the year 2016, the Company's senior officers execute supervision requirements by the supervision authorities, strictly execute resolutions by the Board of Directors and the Board of Supervisors, are adaptable to influences by weak macroscopic growth, accelerated market-oriented interest rate and policies on removal of excessive productive capacity, removal of stock and cost lowering, increase efficiency of the root layer, advance concentration business, supplement disadvantages for development, deepen reform and innovation and realize the objectives of not breaking through the asset safety prevention and control baseline, not occurring significant cases and keeping all supervision indexes in conformity with all requirements.

## X. Report by the Board of Supervisors

### 3 Key work of the Board of Supervisors in year 2017

(1) Continue to reinforce supervision over risk management and control. According to the general requirements of “focusing on risk management, control and dissolving than ever”, establish the baseline philosophy, reinforce crisis awareness, closely focus on and supervise risk control related to the Company’s development, further establish and perfect the overall risk management system, and supervise implementation of policies, procedures and measures by the Board of Directors and operation tier for key issues. Supervise implementation of risk prevention and control measures by the operation tier in the entire risk prevention and control. Highlight the prudence management philosophy, further reinforce supervision over case prevention and control, liquidity risks and operation continuity, and promote safe, stable and healthy operation of the Bank. Reinforce supervision over financial management, supervise execution of financial budget by the operation tier as approved by the Board of Directors, execution of financial revenues and expenditures by branches delivered by the head office, use and management of allocated fund and authenticity of annual profits, and reinforce supervision and check over key project expenditures.

(2) Reinforce assessment on and supervision over performance of directors and senior officers. Further improve assessment report system for directors and senior officers of business tier, observe, supervise and assess performance in multiple dimensions, and propose supervision and assessment opinions and work improvement opinions. Reinforce sufficient communication with directors and senior officers, fully support work by the Board of Directors and business tier, provide suggestions for work of the Bank, and advance operation and management of the Company to a new level.

(3) Continue to reinforce supervision over implementation of key and significant policies. Assess scientific nature, reasonableness and effectiveness of development strategy and risk control strategy drafted by the Board of Directors, on the basis of external situations of economic and social development under the new normal, overall situations of financial field reform and intrinsic conditions for the Company's reform and development, and focus on implementation of capital supplementation for a new round of capital increasing and share expansion, listing and operation of consumption financial companies and other key strategies. Focus on operation and management, advance business transformation and lean management, check implementation of medium, small and pretty financial business and individual financial business transformation policy, performance management policy and effects, and information science& technology governance, safety operation, maintenance and management.

(4) Continue to reinforce supervision performance of the Board of Supervisors. Fully play the spirits of approaching reality, combine work of the Board of Supervisors and daily management operation, start with reinforcing self-restriction mechanism, improve work and increase operation efficiency by organizing special audit, check, investigation and tracking inspection.

(5) Continue to reinforce building of the Board of Supervisors. Find out the foothold for work of the Board of Supervisors under the new normal. According to new requirements on corporate governance system by the State-owned Assets Management Committee and the supervision authorities, perfect organization structure of the Board of Supervisors, clarify duties and authorities of the Board of Supervisors, establish and perfect supervision and management systems, and assess duty performance by supervisors. Focus on communication and cooperation with Party Committee, the Board of Directors and Senior Management, reinforce liaison with departments of head office and branches, and play the role of "taking opinions and proposing suggestions". Besides, reinforce reporting and communicating with the supervision authorities, try to acquire business guide and working support from the supervision authorities, carefully take opinions from the supervision authorities, introduce advanced philosophy, and further improve business performance and professionalism of the Board of Directors.

## 十二、财务报告

本公司2016年度财务报告经青海大正会计师事务所有限公司注册会计师吴海泉、何玉寿根据中国注册会计师独立审计准则，实施了审计工作，审计报告和经审计确认后的财务会计报表如下：

### 审计报告

#### 青海银行股份有限公司：

我们审计了后附的青海银行股份有限公司(以下简称贵公司)财务报表，包括2016年12月31日的资产负债表，2016年度的利润表、现金流量表和股东权益变动表以及财务报表附注。

## 一、管理层对财务报表的责任

编制和公允列报财务报表是贵公司管理层的责任。这种责任包括：(1)按照企业会计准则的规定编制财务报表，并使其实现公允反映；(2)设计、执行和维护必要的内部控制，以使财务报表不存在由于舞弊或错误而导致的重大错报。

## 二、注册会计师责任

我们的责任是在执行审计工作的基础上对财务报表发表审计意见。我们按照中国注册会计师审计准则的规定计划执行了审计工作。中国注册会计师审计准则要求我们遵守中国注册会计师职业道德守则，计划和执行审计工作以对财务报表是否不存在重大错报获取合理保证。

审计工作涉及实施审计程序，以获取有关财务报表金额和披露的审计证据。选择的审计程序取决于注册会计师的判断，包括对由于舞弊或错误导致的财务报表重大错报风险的评估。在进行风险评估时，注册会计师考虑与财务报表编制和公允列报相关的内部控制，以设计恰当的审计程序，但目的并非对内部控制的有效性发表意见。审计工作还包括评价管理层选用会计政策的恰当性和作出会计估计的合理性，以及评价财务报表的总体列报。

我们相信，我们获取的审计证据是充分、适当的，为发表审计意见提供了基础。

## 三、审计意见

我们认为，青海银行股份有限公司财务报表在所有重大方面按照企业会计准则的规定编制，公允反映了贵公司2016年12月31日的财务状况以及2016年度的经营成果和现金流量。

XII. Financial Report

The Company’s financial report for 2016 has been audited by CPA of Qinghai Dazheng CPA Co., Ltd. Wu Haiquan and He Yushou in accordance with the independent audit principle of CPA of China, and the audit report and the audited and confirmed financial accounting statements are given as follows:

# Audit Report

##### BANK OF QINGHAI CO., LTD.

We have audited the attached financial report forms of Bank of Qinghai Co., Ltd. (hereafter referred to as the Company), including balance sheet on December 31, 2016, profit sheet, statement of change in shareholder equity, cash flow sheet and annotation for financial reporting for 2016.

### I. Responsibility for accounting reporting forms by the management

Preparation and fair disclosure of financial statement are your Company management's responsibilities, which include (1) prepare financial statements in accordance with the corporate accounting code to be reflected fairly; (2) design, execute and maintain necessary internal control to make the financial statement free of any significant errors due to embezzlement or errors.

### II. CPA's responsibility

Our responsibility is to make comment based on auditing the reporting form. We have performed according to auditing principle for China's CPA, which requires us to observe vocational and moral standard, plan and perform audit and without key mistake.

Audit involves audit proof and audited amount and disclosed audit proof. The selected audit procedure depends on CPA's judge, including assessment on possible key mistake by embezzlement or mistake. When assessing risk, CPA will consider relevant internal control related to compilation of the reporting form and appropriate audit procedure, but not to make comment on validity of internal control. Audit also includes correction of accounting policy and reasonability of accounting estimate and comment on the whole reporting forms.

We believe that the audit proof obtained by us is sufficient and reasonable and paves the way for making audit comment.

### III. Audit comment

We think, the financial statements of the Bank of Qinghai in all significant aspects in accordance with the Accounting Standards for Enterprise has been compiling the provisions, fairly reflects financial situations of your company dated on December 31, 2016, business achievement and cash flow of the year 2016.

Qinghai Dazheng Certified Public Account Co., Ltd.
